Second transcription's note: Each STOP that says to check line 27c counts only if that question is reached in the flowchart order. The nonresident-alien STOP is refused elsewhere. The Worksheet B STOPs do not apply because Worksheet B is refused.

Ruling: representation and scope; project retained; equivalence not proved. The second transcription writes the line 27c instructions in its own structure; its only substantive addition is the spouse's SSN on a joint return (married filing statuses are refused). The project's line counts each STOP that says to check line 27c only where the flow chart reaches it, plus Worksheet A line 2 zero; ten Stage 9 fixtures assert it, each predicted by hand.

Ruling: equivalent representation; project retained. The same choice (line 2 when line 4 is Yes or line 3 is below the line 5 amount; the smaller of lines 5 and 2 otherwise) written as two guards instead of nested choices.

## Refusal gates listed by the second transcription

Reported, not judged: the project often refuses before a worksheet is used, on the line that chooses it, which a per-line comparison cannot see.

| line | gates listed | not read by the project's line |
| --- | --- | --- |
| eic.credit | eic.clergy, f8959.unsupported, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | eic.clergy, f8959.unsupported |
| eic.check_27c | eic.clergy, f8959.unsupported, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | eic.clergy, f8959.unsupported |
| (Step 5, 1) | eic.clergy | eic.clergy |
| (Step 5, 2) | scope.other_income | - |
| (Step 5, 3) | eic.clergy, scope.other_income | eic.clergy |
| (Step 5, 4) | s8812.combat_pay | - |
| (Step 5, 5) | eic.clergy, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income | eic.clergy |
| 1 | eic.clergy, f8959.unsupported, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | eic.clergy, f8959.unsupported |
| 2 | eic.clergy, f8959.unsupported, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | eic.clergy, f8959.unsupported |
| 4 | eic.clergy, f8959.unsupported, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | eic.clergy, f8959.unsupported |
| 6 | eic.clergy, f8959.unsupported, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | eic.clergy, f8959.unsupported |

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  # ---------- shared helpers ----------
  - id: eic.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}

  - id: eic.h_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Filing a joint return (married filing jointly)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}

  - id: eic.h_mfs_hoh
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Filing status is married filing separately or head of household"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "hoh"}]}

  - id: eic.h_married_sep
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "A married taxpayer whose filing status is married filing separately or head of household (Step 3, question 3; Step 4, question 1)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [eic.married, eic.h_mfs_hoh]}

  - id: eic.h_qc_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of children who meet the conditions to be your qualifying child for the EIC (Step 3, question 1), with or without a valid SSN"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: dependent, field: dependent.one, where: dependent.eic_child}
    refuses: [dependent.other_claimant, dependent.married, dependent.divorced_rule]
    note: "The tie-breaker rules for a qualifying child of more than one person, and the married-child rule, are not computed. This line refuses those situations through the per-dependent screening inputs."

  - id: eic.h_has_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Do you have at least one child who meets the conditions to be your qualifying child for the purpose of claiming the EIC? (Step 3, question 1)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 0}]}

  - id: eic.h_ssn_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of qualifying children who have valid SSNs"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: dependent, field: dependent.one, where: dependent.eic_ssn_child}

  # ---------- income limits by number of children with valid SSNs ----------
  - id: eic.h_lim0_o
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$19,104 limit, no children with valid SSNs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit0.other}

  - id: eic.h_lim0_j
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$26,214 limit if married filing jointly, no children with valid SSNs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit0.joint}

  - id: eic.h_lim1_o
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$50,434 limit, 1 child with a valid SSN"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit1.other}

  - id: eic.h_lim1_j
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$57,554 limit if married filing jointly, 1 child with a valid SSN"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit1.joint}

  - id: eic.h_lim2_o
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$57,310 limit, 2 children with valid SSNs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit2.other}

  - id: eic.h_lim2_j
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$64,430 limit if married filing jointly, 2 children with valid SSNs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit2.joint}

  - id: eic.h_lim3_o
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$61,555 limit, 3 or more children with valid SSNs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit3.other}

  - id: eic.h_lim3_j
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$68,675 limit if married filing jointly, 3 or more children with valid SSNs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit3.joint}

  - id: eic.h_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Income limit for your number of children with valid SSNs and filing status (Step 1, question 1; Step 5, question 3)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, all: [eic.h_joint, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_ssn_count, {const: 0}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim0_j
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, not: eic.h_joint}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_ssn_count, {const: 0}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim0_o
        - guard: {op: test, all: [eic.h_joint, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_ssn_count, {const: 1}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim1_j
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, not: eic.h_joint}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_ssn_count, {const: 1}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim1_o
        - guard: {op: test, all: [eic.h_joint, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_ssn_count, {const: 2}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim2_j
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, not: eic.h_joint}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_ssn_count, {const: 2}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim2_o
        - guard: {op: test, all: [eic.h_joint, {op: test, ge: [eic.h_ssn_count, {const: 3}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim3_j
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, not: eic.h_joint}, {op: test, ge: [eic.h_ssn_count, {const: 3}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim3_o

  # ---------- Step 1 ----------
  - id: eic.h_s1_agi_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1, question 1: is the amount on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b, less than the limit for the number of children with valid SSNs who lived with you?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [f1040.line11b, eic.h_limit]}
    note: "Step 1 counts children with valid SSNs who lived with you. This line uses the count of qualifying children with valid SSNs (dependent.eic_ssn_child) for that count."

  - id: eic.h_spouse_ssn
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "If filing a joint return, does your spouse have a social security number issued on or before the due date of your 2025 return (including extensions) that allows work and is valid for EIC purposes?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: input, source: "Step 1, question 2 (spouse)"}
    note: "No existing input covers the spouse's SSN. This input was added, and it is consulted only on joint returns."

  - id: eic.h_s1_ssn
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1, question 2: do you, and your spouse if filing a joint return, have an SSN issued on or before the due date of your 2025 return (including extensions) that allows you to work and is valid for EIC purposes?"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - household.taxpayer_work_ssn
        - {op: test, any: [{op: test, not: eic.h_joint}, eic.h_spouse_ssn]}
    refuses: [sa.excluded_income, scope.other_income]
    note: "household.taxpayer_work_ssn asks about an SSN valid for employment issued before the due date, which is used here as the EIC-valid SSN. Step 1, question 3 (Form 2555) is refused through sa.excluded_income. Question 4 (nonresident alien) is refused through scope.other_income."

  # ---------- Step 2 ----------
  - id: eic.h_7a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 1040 line 7a; if line 7a is a loss, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: larger_of, of: [f1040.line7a, eic.h_zero]}

  - id: eic.h_invinc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 2, question 1: Investment income (Form 1040 lines 2a + 2b + 3b + 7a)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line2a, f1040.line2b, f1040.line3b, eic.h_7a]}

  - id: eic.h_inv_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 2, question 2: is your investment income $11,950 or less?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, le: [eic.h_invinc, {param: eic.investment_limit}]}
    refuses: [sd.other_forms, scope.schedule1, scope.other_line16]
    note: "Form 4797 (question 3) is refused through sd.other_forms. Without Form 4797, investment income over $11,950 means STOP. The question 4 situations (Schedule E, personal-property rental, Form 8814, passive activity) are refused."

  # ---------- Step 3 ----------
  - id: eic.h_s3_pass
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3 passed for a filer with a qualifying child (questions 2 through 6)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - eic.h_joint
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, not: eic.qc_of_another}
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, not: eic.h_married_sep}
                - {op: test, not: eic.same_home}
                - eic.legally_separated

  - id: eic.separated_box
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box in the Dependents section for the special rule for separated spouses"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - eic.h_has_qc
        - {op: test, not: eic.h_joint}
        - eic.h_married_sep
        - {op: test, any: [{op: test, not: eic.same_home}, eic.legally_separated]}
        - {op: test, not: eic.qc_of_another}
    note: "Step 3, question 6 says 'No' leads to checking the separated-spouses box. Per the intermediate-value definition, this line is limited to married MFS/HOH filers who passed questions 4 and 5."

  # ---------- Step 4 ----------
  - id: eic.h_spouse_age
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "If filing a joint return, was your spouse born after December 31, 1960, and before January 2, 2001?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: input, source: "Step 4, question 2 (spouse)"}
    note: "No existing input covers the spouse's age. This input was added, and it is consulted only on joint returns."

  - id: eic.h_age_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4, question 2: were you, or your spouse if filing a joint return, at least age 25 but under age 65 at the end of 2025?"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, all: [eic.born_before_2001, {op: test, not: sa.age65}]}
        - {op: test, all: [eic.h_joint, eic.h_spouse_age]}
    note: "Uses born before January 2, 2001 (eic.born_before_2001) and not born before January 2, 1961 (sa.age65). The Pub. 596 rule for a decedent is not modeled."

  - id: eic.h_s4_pass
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4 passed for a filer without a qualifying child"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, not: eic.h_married_sep}
        - eic.h_age_ok
        - eic.us_home
        - {op: test, any: [eic.h_joint, {op: test, not: eic.qc_of_another}]}
    refuses: [sa.dependent]
    note: "Step 4, question 6 (can be claimed as a dependent) is refused through sa.dependent."

  # ---------- Step 5 worksheet ----------
  - id: eic.clergy
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Are you filing Schedule SE because you were a member of the clergy or you had church employee income of $108.28 or more, with an amount also reported on Form 1040, line 1z?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: input, source: "Step 5, question 1; line 27b"}

  - id: "eic.line(Step 5, 1)"
    printed: "(Step 5, 1)"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 1z"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line1z}
    refuses: [eic.clergy]

  - id: "eic.line(Step 5, 2)"
    printed: "(Step 5, 2)"
    label: "Enter the Medicaid waiver payment amounts excluded from income on Schedule 1, line 8s, unless you choose to include these amounts in earned income, in which case enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}
    refuses: [scope.other_income]

  - id: "eic.line(Step 5, 3)"
    printed: "(Step 5, 3)"
    label: "Subtract line 2 from line 1"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: "eic.line(Step 5, 1)", take: "eic.line(Step 5, 2)"}

  - id: "eic.line(Step 5, 4)"
    printed: "(Step 5, 4)"
    label: "Enter all of your nontaxable combat pay if you elect to include it in earned income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}
    refuses: [s8812.combat_pay]

  - id: "eic.line(Step 5, 5)"
    printed: "(Step 5, 5)"
    label: "Add lines 3 and 4. This is your earned income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: ["eic.line(Step 5, 3)", "eic.line(Step 5, 4)"]}

  - id: eic.h_s5_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 5, question 3: is your earned income less than the limit for your number of qualifying children with valid SSNs?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: ["eic.line(Step 5, 5)", eic.h_limit]}

  - id: eic.h_pass
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Steps 1 through 5 passed"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - eic.h_s1_agi_ok
        - eic.h_s1_ssn
        - eic.h_inv_ok
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, all: [eic.h_has_qc, eic.h_s3_pass]}
            - {op: test, all: [{op: test, not: eic.h_has_qc}, eic.h_s4_pass]}
        - eic.h_s5_ok
    note: "Step 6 (asking the IRS to figure the credit) is not asked. Worksheet A is always used."

  # ---------- Worksheet A ----------
  - id: eic.line1
    printed: "1"
    label: "Enter your earned income from Step 5"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: "eic.line(Step 5, 5)"}
    refuses: [eic.clergy, f8959.unsupported, scope.schedule1]
    note: "Worksheet A applies only if the answer to Step 5, question 2 is No. Self-employment, clergy and church employee income, and statutory-employee Schedule C income all require Worksheet B and are refused."

  - id: eic.line2
    printed: "2"
    label: "Look up the amount on line 1 above in the EIC Table to find the credit. Be sure you use the correct column for your filing status and the number of qualifying children you have who have valid SSNs. Enter the credit here."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status, children: eic.h_ssn_count}
    note: "The grammar's table_lookup has no children operand, so a 'children' key was added because the EIC Table is keyed by the number of qualifying children with valid SSNs (0, 1, 2, 3 or more). Married filing separately uses the Single/HOH/QSS column (table star note)."

  - id: eic.line3
    printed: "3"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line11b}

  - id: eic.line4
    printed: "4"
    label: "Are the amounts on lines 3 and 1 the same?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [eic.line3, eic.line1]}

  - id: eic.h_ws_threshold
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 threshold: $10,620 ($17,730 MFJ) with no qualifying children with valid SSNs; $23,350 ($30,470 MFJ) with 1 or more"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, all: [eic.h_joint, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_ssn_count, {const: 0}]}]}
          then: eic.h_wst0_j
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, not: eic.h_joint}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_ssn_count, {const: 0}]}]}
          then: eic.h_wst0_o
        - guard: {op: test, all: [eic.h_joint, {op: test, ge: [eic.h_ssn_count, {const: 1}]}]}
          then: eic.h_wst1_j
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, not: eic.h_joint}, {op: test, ge: [eic.h_ssn_count, {const: 1}]}]}
          then: eic.h_wst1_o

  - id: eic.h_wst0_o
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$10,620"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold0.other}

  - id: eic.h_wst0_j
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$17,730 if married filing jointly"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold0.joint}

  - id: eic.h_wst1_o
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$23,350"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold1.other}

  - id: eic.h_wst1_j
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$30,470 if married filing jointly"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold1.joint}

  - id: eic.h_use5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 4 is No and line 3 is not less than the line 5 threshold (line 5 is used)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, not: eic.line4}
        - {op: test, ge: [eic.line3, eic.h_ws_threshold]}

  - id: eic.line5
    printed: "5"
    label: "Look up the amount on line 3 in the EIC Table to find the credit. Be sure you use the correct column for your filing status and the number of qualifying children you have who have valid SSNs. Enter the credit here."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status, children: eic.h_ssn_count}
    note: "Line 5 is printed as left blank or skipped unless eic.h_use5 is true. The lookup is always computed but is used on line 6 only when eic.h_use5 is true. A line 3 amount at or above the table's end is read as $0."

  - id: eic.h_smaller52
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Look at the amounts on lines 5 and 2. Then, enter the smaller amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [eic.line5, eic.line2]}

  - id: eic.line6
    printed: "6"
    label: "This is your earned income credit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: eic.h_use5, then: eic.h_smaller52}
        - {guard: {op: test, not: eic.h_use5}, then: eic.line2}

  # ---------- outputs ----------
  - id: eic.credit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned income credit for Form 1040, line 27a"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: eic.h_pass, then: eic.line6}
        - {guard: {op: test, not: eic.h_pass}, then: eic.h_zero}

  - id: eic.h_reach3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Reached Step 3 (Steps 1 and 2 passed)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [eic.h_s1_agi_ok, eic.h_s1_ssn, eic.h_inv_ok]}

  - id: eic.h_reach4
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Reached Step 4, question 3 (no qualifying child; questions 1 and 2 passed)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - eic.h_reach3
        - {op: test, not: eic.h_has_qc}
        - {op: test, not: eic.h_married_sep}
        - eic.h_age_ok

  - id: eic.check_27c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box on line 27c (instructed by a STOP in the line 27a instructions)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, all: [eic.h_s1_agi_ok, {op: test, not: eic.h_s1_ssn}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - eic.h_reach3
            - eic.h_has_qc
            - {op: test, not: eic.h_joint}
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, not: eic.h_married_sep}
                - {op: test, not: eic.same_home}
                - eic.legally_separated
            - eic.qc_of_another
        - {op: test, all: [eic.h_reach4, {op: test, not: eic.us_home}]}
        - {op: test, all: [eic.h_reach4, eic.us_home, {op: test, not: eic.h_joint}, eic.qc_of_another]}
        - {op: test, all: [eic.h_pass, {op: test, eq: [eic.line2, {const: 0}]}]}
    note: "Each STOP that says to check line 27c counts only if that question is reached in the flowchart order. The nonresident-alien STOP is refused elsewhere. The Worksheet B STOPs do not apply because Worksheet B is refused."
```
```
